) AS [r1]

    SELECT [r0].[RouteStopId], [r0].[PlannedArrivalTime], [r0].[RouteId], [r0].[StopId], [r0].[StopOrder], [s].[StopId] AS [StopId0], [s].[CompanyId], [s].[CreatedAt], [s].[Latitude], [s].[Longitude], [s].[Status], [s].[StopName]
